# Card review sheet

Generated 2026-10-31 by `npm run cards:review`. Do not edit by hand — edit the
source modules under `src/content` and regenerate. Each row is one card;
the Review column is intentionally blank so you can mark it up.

| ☐ | Trade Evolution | `pokemon.route-trade-evolution` | group | Swap drinks with the player across from you, then both drink 1. Trade evolution! |  |

Hand out 10. 20-20: Natural 20! A clean hit. Hand out 20. · Beat 2: His last Pokémon beats your starter. Of course it does. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Smell ya later. Everyone drinks 8. 2-10: Smell ya later. Everyone drinks 4. 11-19: Blue's out. Everyone else drinks 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Blue's out. Everyone else drinks 8.) |

Red nods once. Give 12.) |

## Dungeon night (`dungeon`)

575 cards · 36 sip · 97 group · 0 category · 404 challenge · 38 rule · 288 dice

| Review | Title | ID | Cat | Rules | Dice, secret or timer |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ☐ | Lost and Found | `dungeon.lost-and-found` | sip | Everything anyone lost down here, plus a coaster that looks bulletproof. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Tip Jar | `dungeon.tip-jar` | sip | A tip jar lost under the shelves since 2011. A loaded die rattles in the coins. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Rat Fence | `dungeon.rat-fence` | sip | A rat in a tiny vest is selling whatever fell behind the bar. He takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Old Regular | `dungeon.the-regular` | sip | He's been down here since the cellar was a bar. He'll trade a story for a story. |  |
| ☐ | Cellar Master's Coat | `dungeon.cellar-masters-coat` | sip | A coat on a hook with full pockets: a corkscrew and a smoke bomb. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Magnum | `dungeon.the-magnum` | sip | A magnum of something nice behind the racks. Drink 1, and cork the rest for later. |  |
| ☐ | The Cellar Cat | `dungeon.cellar-cat` | sip | A cat in a sommelier's chain trades mouse-bitten goods. It takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Vintner | `dungeon.the-vintner` | sip | A tiny man in a huge hat sells you a vintage. He insists you taste first. |  |
| ☐ | The Bone Pile | `dungeon.bone-pile` | sip | Under the bones: a smoke bomb and a stranger's fake ID. Keep both. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Offering Bowl | `dungeon.offering-bowl` | sip | A bowl of coins left for the dead, and a draught nobody touched. Take it. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Grave Robber | `dungeon.grave-robber` | sip | A grave robber with a cart of stolen goods and no questions asked. Prices are firm. |  |
| ☐ | The Undertaker | `dungeon.the-undertaker` | sip | He measures you for a coffin while he sells you things. He's very thorough. |  |
| ☐ | Flushed Purse | `dungeon.flushed-purse` | sip | A purse dropped down a toilet, somehow dry: lipstick, a lighter, an open bar tab. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Maintenance Locker | `dungeon.maintenance-locker` | sip | A hard hat on a hook, and a thermos of something brown. Drink 1 from the thermos. |  |
| ☐ | The Mole Man | `dungeon.mole-man` | sip | A man who lives down here sells whatever washes past. He takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Rat Pharmacist | `dungeon.rat-pharmacist` | sip | A rat in a white coat with a briefcase of pills, potions and one very old book. |  |
| ☐ | Message in a Bottle | `dungeon.message-bottle` | sip | It says drink me. Of course it does. Drink 1, and keep the bottle. |  |
| ☐ | The Lifebuoy | `dungeon.lifebuoy` | sip | A lifebuoy off the wall, still inflated. Wear it with pride. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Pearl Diver | `dungeon.pearl-diver` | sip | A diver in a rusted helmet trades whatever he finds at the bottom of the bar. |  |
| ☐ | The Ferryman | `dungeon.the-ferryman` | sip | A hooded figure in a pedal boat takes you across, for a price, and sells extras. |  |
| ☐ | Staff Meal | `dungeon.staff-meal` | sip | Fries, pickles, and a shot the cooks hid for you. Drink 1, and pocket a smoke bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Knife Roll | `dungeon.knife-roll` | sip | A chef's knife roll left on the pass, something heavy in the end pocket. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Dishwasher | `dungeon.the-dishwasher` | sip | The dishwasher has seen everything, and he'll sell you some of it, for drinks or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Sous Chef | `dungeon.the-sous-chef` | sip | The sous chef is selling the head chef's stash, quietly, out the back. |  |
| ☐ | Bottle Cap Stash | `dungeon.bottle-cap-stash` | sip | A jar of bottle caps, and a loaded die someone hid in it. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Growler | `dungeon.the-growler` | sip | A full growler on a shelf, sealed, labeled YOURS. Drink 1, and keep it. |  |
| ☐ | The Apprentice | `dungeon.apprentice-brewer` | sip | The brewmaster's apprentice is selling off the good stuff while the boss is out. |  |
| ☐ | The Hop Dealer | `dungeon.hop-dealer` | sip | He sells hops out of a trench coat and wants you to agree his beer is best. |  |
| ☐ | The Lost Parka | `dungeon.lost-parka` | sip | A parka on a hook, a hip flask in the pocket. Drink 1, and keep the flask. |  |
| ☐ | The Ice Tray | `dungeon.ice-tray` | sip | An ice tray, every cube frozen around a coin. One of them is a smoke bomb. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Yeti | `dungeon.the-yeti` | sip | A yeti in a cardigan runs a little shop out of the ice cream aisle. |  |
| ☐ | The Frozen Mime | `dungeon.the-frozen-mime` | sip | A mime who got stuck in here mid-act. He sells by pointing. |  |
| ☐ | Left Behind | `dungeon.rooftop-lost-found` | sip | A box behind the bar: one shoe, one earring and a smoke bomb. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Abandoned Table | `dungeon.abandoned-table` | sip | A whole table left for the bathroom and never came back. Their bottle is yours. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Rooftop Bartender | `dungeon.the-rooftop-bartender` | sip | A bartender in a linen shirt selling $24 cocktails, and she takes blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Bottle Girl | `dungeon.the-bottle-girl` | sip | A sparkler bottle, a spreadsheet of tables, and a spot in the corner, for a price. |  |

| ☐ | The Dead Jukebox | `dungeon.dead-jukebox` | group | It plays one song, badly, forever. Everyone sings the chorus, whatever they think it is. |  |

| ☐ | Staff Only | `dungeon.staff-only` | group | Two doors: STAFF ONLY and REALLY STAFF ONLY. The second one growls. |  |

| ☐ | Mystery Bottle | `dungeon.cellar-mystery-bottle` | group | A dusty bottle, no label. Everyone guesses what's in it; the drawer's favorite guess gives 2. |  |
| ☐ | Spider Check | `dungeon.cellar-spider-check` | group | Everyone checks their hair for spiders, loudly. The last to finish drinks 1. |  |
| ☐ | Dry Storage | `dungeon.cellar-dry-storage` | group | You find a crate of crackers. Everyone eats one or drinks 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Clean Glass | `dungeon.cellar-last-glass` | group | Bid sips for the last clean glass, clockwise, until everyone passes. Top bid drinks it and toasts. |  |
| ☐ | The Tasting Room | `dungeon.tasting-room` | group | Cheese, crackers, and one clean glass. Everyone toasts the cheese. |  |
| ☐ | The Candle Nook | `dungeon.candle-nook` | group | A nook with a hundred melted candles. Everyone makes a wish and blows out a pretend one. |  |
| ☐ | Red or White | `dungeon.red-or-white` | group | Two aisles. White is quiet. Red is where the screaming is coming from. |  |
| ☐ | The Bung Hole | `dungeon.the-bung-hole` | group | A barrel big enough to crawl through, or the stairs. Something inside is giggling. |  |

| ☐ | Proper Cheers | `dungeon.wine-cheers` | group | Everyone clinks with everyone, eyes locked, or it's bad luck. Anyone who skips someone drinks 1. |  |
| ☐ | Vintage | `dungeon.wine-best-year` | group | Everyone names their best year, fast, around the table. Repeat a year or stall: drink 2. |  |

| ☐ | Candlelight | `dungeon.catacombs-candlelight` | group | Phone lights up for a moment of silence for the fallen. Anyone who talks drinks 1. |  |
| ☐ | Epitaphs | `dungeon.catacombs-epitaphs` | group | Everyone says their own epitaph in five words. The table picks a favorite; they give 3. |  |

| ☐ | The Compressor | `dungeon.the-compressor` | group | Warm air blowing out of a vent. Everyone huddles in and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Hot Chocolate | `dungeon.hot-chocolate` | group | A thermos of hot chocolate, still warm, with a note: FOR WHOEVER'S NEXT. Everyone sips, slowly. |  |
| ☐ | Two Shelves | `dungeon.two-shelves` | group | Behind the frozen peas, or through the ice cream aisle, where something licks. |  |
| ☐ | The Meat Hooks | `dungeon.the-meat-hooks` | group | Duck under the empty hooks, or push through the full ones, swinging on their own. |  |
| ☐ | Penguin Walk | `dungeon.freezer-penguin-walk` | group | The drawer waddles like a penguin to the nearest wall and back. Refuse: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Cold Take | `dungeon.freezer-cold-take` | group | Everyone shares their coldest take. The table picks the coldest; they give 3. |  |
| ☐ | Huddle Up | `dungeon.freezer-huddle` | group | Everyone huddles in for warmth. Anyone who won't drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Hot Cocoa | `dungeon.freezer-hot-cocoa` | group | Everyone warms their hands on their drink and takes a slow, cozy sip. |  |
| ☐ | Cold Takes | `dungeon.freezer-cold-takes` | group | Everyone shares a cold take. The table boos the coldest one; its owner drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The View | `dungeon.the-view` | group | The whole city, lit up below. Everyone stops, looks, and drinks to it. |  |
| ☐ | The Fire Pit | `dungeon.the-fire-pit` | group | A fire pit and a ring of chairs. Everyone sits closer than they need to and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Two Terraces | `dungeon.the-two-terraces` | group | The quiet terrace with fairy lights, or the loud one with the private party. |  |
| ☐ | The Open Window | `dungeon.the-fire-escape` | group | Take the stairs across, or climb through the open window, where someone is shouting. |  |
| ☐ | Skyline Toast | `dungeon.rooftop-skyline-toast` | group | Everyone toasts something they can see from here. Repeat a toast: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Shooting Star | `dungeon.rooftop-shooting-star` | group | The drawer makes a wish out loud. If the table thinks it's lame, the drawer drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Pigeon | `dungeon.rooftop-pigeon` | group | A pigeon lands on the table. Everyone coos; the worst coo drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Fresh Air | `dungeon.rooftop-fresh-air` | group | Everyone takes a deep breath of fresh air. Then a sip. |  |
| ☐ | First to Post | `dungeon.rooftop-first-to-post` | group | On three, everyone points at who will post this roof first. Most fingers: give 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Stairwell | `dungeon.landing-stairwell` | group | A cold stairwell and one flickering bulb. Everyone stretches. Level up, or sit down. |  |
| ☐ | The Smoking Area | `dungeon.landing-smoking-area` | group | Fresh air, a dead heater, somebody's vape. Everyone names their best moment so far. |  |
| ☐ | The Coat Check | `dungeon.landing-coat-check` | group | An empty coat check. Everyone names the coat they'd steal. Level up, or nap on the pile. |  |
| ☐ | The Bathroom Line | `dungeon.landing-bathroom-line` | group | Twelve deep, and everyone's best friends now. Everyone hypes their left neighbor. |  |
| ☐ | The Loading Dock | `dungeon.landing-loading-dock` | group | Pallets, a puddle, one working vending machine. Everyone names the snack they'd get. |  |
| ☐ | The Phone Booth | `dungeon.landing-phone-booth` | group | An old phone booth, and the phone works. Everyone says who they'd call right now. |  |
| ☐ | The Mop Closet | `dungeon.landing-mop-closet` | group | Mops, buckets, one comfy bucket. Everyone gives their left neighbor a wrestling name. |  |
| ☐ | The Photo Booth | `dungeon.landing-photo-booth` | group | A broken photo booth that still flashes. Everyone pulls their best face on three. |  |
| ☐ | The Boiler Room | `dungeon.landing-boiler-room` | group | Warm pipes and somebody's cot. Everyone warms their hands. Level up, or lie down. |  |
| ☐ | A Campfire | `dungeon.landing-campfire` | group | Someone lit a fire in a keg. Everyone tells the scariest thing that's happened tonight. |  |
| ☐ | The Staff Room | `dungeon.landing-staff-room` | group | A kettle and a sign: WASH YOUR OWN MUG. Everyone mimes washing one. |  |
| ☐ | The Fire Escape | `dungeon.landing-fire-escape` | group | Stars, a city, and the street far below. Everyone toasts how far you've come. |  |
| ☐ | The Service Stairs | `dungeon.landing-service-stairs` | group | Halfway down, a step wide enough to sit on. Everyone says what they'd order right now. |  |
| ☐ | The Bunker | `dungeon.landing-the-bunker` | group | An old bomb shelter with canned beans. Everyone names who they'd want down here. |  |
| ☐ | The Laundry Room | `dungeon.landing-laundry-room` | group | Warm dryers humming. Everyone confesses their worst laundry crime. |  |
| ☐ | The Stuck Elevator | `dungeon.landing-stuck-elevator` | group | An elevator stuck between floors, the music still playing. Everyone hums along on three. |  |
| ☐ | Gelatinous Shot | `dungeon.gelatinous-shot` | challenge | A wobbling cube of jello shots, a tooth floating in it. Hands behind your back. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You find the tooth. It's yours now. Drink 6, no hands. 2-6: It engulfs you. Drink 3, no hands. 7-19: You eat your way out. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the whole thing, tooth and all. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Rats in a Coat | `dungeon.rat-trench-coat` | challenge | Nine rats in a coat get carded. Left neighbor bounces: talk your way in, 10 seconds. Out: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Mold Elemental | `dungeon.mold-elemental` | challenge | The thing behind the ice machine has feelings and a mouth. Hold your breath and roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It moves into your lungs. Drink 6 and cough on both neighbors. 2-6: You breathe it in. Drink 3 and cough on your left neighbor. 7-19: Bleach. So much bleach. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! It thanks you for the bleach. Everyone else holds their breath; first to gasp drinks 6. |
| ☐ | Drunk Goblin | `dungeon.drunk-goblin` | challenge | A goblin who's had nine. He wants to fight you, then hug you. Arms out, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He hugs you and won't let go. Drink 6 with an arm around your right neighbor. 2-6: He wins both. Drink 3 and hug your right neighbor. 7-19: He passes out on you. Loot his pockets. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! He passes out, and his pockets are deep. Give 4, and everyone toasts the goblin. |
| ☐ | Skeleton Barback | `dungeon.skeleton-barback` | challenge | On three, everyone points at who looks like they work here since 1974. Most fingers: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Possessed Mop | `dungeon.possessed-mop` | challenge | A possessed mop sweeps under the table. Last to lift both feet off the floor drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Keg Spider | `dungeon.keg-spider` | challenge | Eight legs, eight beers, zero manners. Wiggle eight fingers at it, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It lays eggs in your drink. Drink 6, then spider-walk to the fridge and back. 2-6: Bitten. Drink 3, and do a spider walk to the fridge and back. 7-19: Squished. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! Squished, and its web was full of coasters. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Tab Goblin | `dungeon.tab-goblin` | challenge | The Tab Goblin wants paying. Your right neighbor plays him: thumb war, best of three. Lose: drink 4. |  |
| ☐ | Goblin Shaman | `dungeon.goblin-shaman` | challenge | A goblin in a bucket hat waves a burnt sage stick at you and chants. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The chant works. Drink 6. 2-6: The chant works. Drink 3. 7-19: You blow out the sage. Give 3 to whoever smells most like it. 20-20: Natural 20! You blow out the sage. Give 6 to whoever smells most like it. · Jinx Goblin Tongue: The jinxed player speaks of themself in the third person. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Cask Bat | `dungeon.cask-bat` | challenge | Light the fuse. Pass a beer clockwise, naming a beer as you pass. The bat hits whoever holds it. | Fuse 12–40 s: The bat swoops. Whoever is holding the beer drinks 3. |
| ☐ | Clipboard Troll | `dungeon.clipboard-troll` | challenge | You're not on the list. Walk heel to toe across the room and back. Wobble: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Riddling Tap | `dungeon.riddling-tap` | challenge | A tap with a face. Your left neighbor asks a riddle: 10 seconds, or drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Stuck Door | `dungeon.stuck-door` | challenge | Brace it shut with your back: wall-sit for 30 seconds. Stand early: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Keg Stand-Off | `dungeon.keg-stand-off` | challenge | Staring contest with your right neighbor. You blink or laugh first: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Sticky Floor | `dungeon.sticky-floor` | challenge | Your shoes are glued to forty years of spilled lager. Roll d20 to pull free. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You leave a shoe behind. Drink 4, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 2-8: You leave a shoe behind. Drink 2, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 9-19: You peel free. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You peel free and moonwalk. Everyone copies it; the worst moonwalk drinks 4. |
| ☐ | Tripwire | `dungeon.christmas-lights` | challenge | Someone strung Christmas lights across the stairs at shin height. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You eat the stairs. Drink 4. 2-8: You eat the stairs. Drink 2. 9-19: You hop it. Give 2 to the clumsiest player here. 20-20: Natural 20! You hop it. Give 4 to the clumsiest player here. |
| ☐ | Rolling Kegs | `dungeon.rolling-kegs` | challenge | The keg rack groans. Then it lets go. Roll d20 to dive. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Flattened. Drink 6, lying down if you can. 2-8: Flattened. Drink 3, lying down if you can. 9-19: You dive clear. Give 2 to whoever didn't flinch. 20-20: Natural 20! You dive clear. Give 4 to whoever didn't flinch. |
| ☐ | Loose Tap Handles | `dungeon.falling-taps` | challenge | A foam tap handle bonks you, and you're Thumb Master now. Drink 1, then use it. | Jinx Thumb Master: Whenever the jinxed player puts a thumb on the table, the last to copy drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Lost Bridesmaids | `dungeon.lost-bridesmaids` | challenge | Two lost bridesmaids have decided you're the groom. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! First dance. Drink 4, then sway with your right neighbor. 2-6: First dance. Drink 2, then sway with your right neighbor. 7-19: You point them upstairs. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You point them upstairs. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Rat Pack | `dungeon.rat-pack` | challenge | Three rats in fedoras sing Sinatra for your stool. Sing them a line back, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They do it their way. Drink 4. 2-6: They do it their way. Drink 2. 7-19: You heckle them offstage. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You heckle them offstage. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Fryer Oil Ooze | `dungeon.fryer-oil-ooze` | challenge | Twenty years of fryer oil got up and walked, and it slides clockwise from you. Roll four dice. | 4d6 — It slicks four seats. You drink {first}; the next three clockwise drink {second}, {third} and {fourth}. |
| ☐ | Barback Poltergeist | `dungeon.barback-poltergeist` | challenge | An invisible barback keeps stacking empty glasses on your head. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: The tower falls. Drink 3. 6-12: You tip him a coin. Stack two empty glasses on someone's head, then give 3. |
| ☐ | Cobweb Bartender | `dungeon.cobweb-bartender` | challenge | Read alone what he poured you, then start the clock and mime drinking it. The table guesses. | Secret: A PICKLEBACK. · Clock 45 s: Time. Someone guessed it? Give 3. Nobody did? Drink 3. |
| ☐ | The Low Beam | `dungeon.low-beam` | challenge | Your right neighbor holds out an arm as the beam. Limbo under it. Touch it: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Loose Floorboard | `dungeon.loose-floorboard` | challenge | One board in the cellar floor is not like the others. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It springs up and smacks you. Drink 4. 2-8: It springs up and smacks you. Drink 2. 9-19: You step over it like a cat. Meow, then give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You step over it like a cat. Meow, then give 4. |
| ☐ | The Stag Party | `dungeon.stag-party` | challenge | Eight men in matching shirts, one in a veil, all looking for a ninth. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Recruited. Drink 4, then give a best-man toast. 2-6: Recruited. Drink 2, then give a best-man toast. 7-19: You send them to the Wine Cellar. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You send them to the Wine Cellar. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Grease Trap Horror | `dungeon.grease-trap-horror` | challenge | The grease trap has been fed fries for twenty years. Now it's hungry. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It slurps you in. Drink 8, then wash your hands in shame. 2-9: It slurps you in. Drink 4, then wash your hands in shame. 10-19: You seal the lid and sit on it. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You seal the lid and sit on it. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Health Inspector | `dungeon.health-inspector` | challenge | Clipboard, hairnet, no soul. He's here to shut the cellar down. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Violation. Drink 8 and name three things wrong with this room. 2-9: Violation. Drink 4 and name three things wrong with this room. 10-19: You bribe him with a keg. He leaves his lucky pen. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You bribe him with a keg. He leaves his lucky pen. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Keg Mimic Queen | `dungeon.keg-mimic-queen` | challenge | Every keg in the rack opens one eye. The big one in the middle opens two. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The whole rack bites. Drink 8. 2-9: The whole rack bites. Drink 4. 10-19: You tap the queen. The rack goes quiet. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You tap the queen. The rack goes quiet. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Exterminator | `dungeon.the-exterminator` | challenge | He's here for the rats, and he's decided you count. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Sprayed. Drink 8, holding your breath. 2-9: Sprayed. Drink 4, holding your breath. 10-19: You hide him in an empty keg. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You hide him in an empty keg. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Keg Golem | `dungeon.keg-golem` | challenge | 800 pounds of tapped lager. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It sits on you. Drink 8. 2-8: It sits on you. Drink 4. 9-19: You pull its plug. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You drink it dry. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It gets back up, foaming. Chug race with your left neighbor, playing the Golem. Lose: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Rat King | `dungeon.rat-king` | challenge | Forty rats in one crown, holding court by the drain. Kiss the ring, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The court finds you delicious. Finish your drink. 2-8: The court finds you guilty. Drink 4. 9-19: You dethrone him. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You dethrone him. Give 10. · Beat 2: The court is in session. Plead your case to the table for 20 seconds; they vote. Guilty: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Royal Decree: Everyone calls the jinxed player Your Majesty. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Landlord | `dungeon.the-landlord` | challenge | He wants three months' rent. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Evicted. Finish your drink. 2-8: Late fee. Drink 4. 9-19: You find a code violation. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You find a code violation. Give 10. · Beat 2: He's back with a clipboard for the inspection. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-6: He finds the mold. Drink 4. 7-12: He finds nothing. Give 4.) · Jinx Rent Due: The jinxed player asks the table's permission before every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Big Cheese | `dungeon.big-cheese` | challenge | A rat in cheese armor. Side bets: thumbs up or down on you; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It sits on your snacks. Drink 8. 2-8: It sits on your snacks. Drink 4. 9-19: You eat through the armor. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the armor and the rat. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It rolls at you, cheese first. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: Flattened. Drink 4. 2-2: Cheese in your hair. Drink 3. 3-3: You dodge. Give 3. 4-4: You take a bite out of it. Give 3. 5-5: It rolls into the wall. Give 4. 6-6: You grate it. Everyone else drinks 2.) |
| ☐ | The Pipe Organ | `dungeon.the-pipe-organ` | challenge | Every pipe in the cellar is playing one chord, and it's coming from a face. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You join the chord. Hum for 10 seconds, then drink 8. 2-8: The chord hits you in the teeth. Drink 4. 9-19: You find the off switch. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You find the off switch. Give 10. · Beat 2: It plays your song. Hum any song; the table has 15 seconds to name it. Nobody does: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Organ Voice: The jinxed player sings everything they say. Speaks normally: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Boxed Wine Golem | `dungeon.boxed-wine-golem` | challenge | A golem of boxed wine, slapping its own bag. Name a boxed wine, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It slaps you with the bag. Drink 6, and slap the table twice. 2-6: It slaps you with the bag. Drink 3, and slap the table twice. 7-19: You pop the spout. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You find the spout and the golem deflates with a sigh. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Cork Imp | `dungeon.cork-imp` | challenge | A tiny imp popping corks at eye level and laughing about it. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: Right in the eye. Drink 3 with one eye shut. 2-2: Right in the drink. Drink 3. 3-3: You duck. Give 2. 4-4: You cork him. Give 3. 5-5: You cork him and shake him. Give 3. 6-6: You pop one back at him. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Zombie Sommelier | `dungeon.zombie-sommelier` | challenge | Read alone what he poured, start the clock, then describe it like a wine snob. The table guesses. | Secret: A BOX OF FRANZIA. · Clock 45 s: Time. Someone guessed it? Give 3. Nobody did? Drink 3. |
| ☐ | Wine Mom Banshee | `dungeon.wine-mom-banshee` | challenge | Scream 'FIVE O'CLOCK SOMEWHERE!' against your left neighbor. The table calls the quieter: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Rosé Slime | `dungeon.rose-slime` | challenge | Pink, sticky, and it says it's not like other slimes. Say something basic, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's exactly like other slimes, and so are you. Drink 6. 2-6: It's exactly like other slimes. Drink 3. 7-19: You pop it. It leaves pink smoke behind. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You pop it. It leaves pink smoke behind. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Ghost in the Decanter | `dungeon.decanter-ghost` | challenge | It's been breathing for a hundred years. So has the wine. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: It breathes on you. Drink 3 and say 'notes of ghost'. 6-12: You jam the stopper in. Give 3 to the snobbiest drinker here. · Jinx Spooked: Nobody may look the jinxed player in the eye. Caught: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Barrel Mimic | `dungeon.barrel-mimic` | challenge | On three, everyone points at who'd faint first if a barrel grew teeth. Most fingers: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | Corkscrew Crab | `dungeon.corkscrew-crab` | challenge | A corkscrew crab scuttles under the table, pinching. Last to lift both feet drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Sangria Sprite | `dungeon.sangria-sprite` | challenge | Your right neighbor throws a mimed orange slice. Catch it in your mouth. Booed: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Vinegar Vampire | `dungeon.vinegar-vampire` | challenge | A vampire who only drinks from bottles that turned. He's been at yours. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He's right, it turned. Drink 6, make the face. 2-6: He's right, it turned. Drink 3, make the face. 7-19: You hold up a cross made of corkscrews. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You hold up a cross made of corkscrews. Give 6. · Jinx Sour Face: The jinxed player pulls a sour face before every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Blind Tasting | `dungeon.blind-tasting` | challenge | Eyes shut. Your left neighbor hands you a drink. Name whose it is. Wrong: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Flamingo | `dungeon.coaster-nose` | challenge | Stand on one leg with your eyes shut for 10 seconds. Wobble or peek: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Swirl, Sniff, Lie | `dungeon.swirl-sniff-lie` | challenge | Review your drink like a sommelier for 15 seconds. Crack a smile: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Toast | `dungeon.the-toast` | challenge | Toast your right neighbor, sincerely, for 20 seconds. Anyone laughs: you drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Falling Rack | `dungeon.falling-rack` | challenge | The wine rack tips, one bottle at a time, like dominoes. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Domino'd. Drink 6. 2-8: Domino'd. Drink 3. 9-19: You catch the last one. Give 2, and hand someone your 'bottle'. 20-20: Natural 20! You catch the last one. Give 4, and hand someone your 'bottle'. |
| ☐ | Merlot Stairs | `dungeon.merlot-stairs` | challenge | The stairs are slick with spilled merlot. Stand up, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Down on your ass, in red. Drink 4 standing up. 2-8: Down on your ass, in red. Drink 2 standing up. 9-19: Not a drop on you. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You slide down the rail like it's 1999. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Sulfite Cloud | `dungeon.sulfite-cloud` | challenge | Light the fuse. Pass a beer clockwise, naming a wine. The headache hits whoever holds it. | Fuse 12–40 s: The headache hits. Whoever is holding the beer drinks 3. |
| ☐ | Cork Mines | `dungeon.cork-mines` | challenge | The floor is covered in corks. Some of them click. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Pop. Drink 4. 2-8: Pop. Drink 2. 9-19: You tiptoe through. Give 2 to whoever walks loudest. 20-20: Natural 20! You tiptoe through. Give 4 to whoever walks loudest. |
| ☐ | Fruit Fly Swarm | `dungeon.fruit-fly-swarm` | challenge | A cloud of fruit flies off the dump bucket, and they brought friends. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! In your mouth. Drink 4 with a hand over your glass. 2-6: In your mouth. Drink 2 with a hand over your glass. 7-19: Swatted. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! Swatted. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Book Club | `dungeon.book-club` | challenge | Four women with one unread book. Name a book you pretended to read, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They have notes. Drink 4. 2-6: They have notes. Drink 2. 7-19: You've read it. They scatter. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You've read it. They scatter. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Cork Goblin | `dungeon.cork-goblin` | challenge | A corkscrew goblin opens a twist-off. Everyone mimes popping a cork; the worst pop drinks 2. |  |
| ☐ | Rosé Elemental | `dungeon.rose-elemental` | challenge | Everyone says 'rosé all day' as a threat. The table picks the scariest; they give 3. |  |
| ☐ | Tannin Troll | `dungeon.tannin-troll` | challenge | It dries out every mouth in the room. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: Your tongue sticks. Drink 3, then say 'tannin' five times fast. 6-12: You out-spit it. Give 3. |
| ☐ | The Palate Test | `dungeon.palate-test` | challenge | Your left neighbor names three wines, one made up. Spot the fake. Wrong: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Sediment Pit | `dungeon.sediment-pit` | challenge | The bottom of the barrel is a pit, and the pit is all dregs. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You wade through. Drink 4, and chew. 2-8: You wade through. Drink 2, and chew. 9-19: You vault it. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You vault it. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Gender Reveal | `dungeon.gender-reveal` | challenge | A lost gender reveal party wants you to guess. They have a smoke cannon. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Wrong guess, and the cannon goes off. Drink 4. 2-6: Wrong guess, and the cannon goes off. Drink 2. 7-19: You leave before the smoke. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You leave before the smoke. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Wine Snob | `dungeon.wine-snob` | challenge | He swirls, he sniffs, he says you'd like something approachable. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Approachable. Drink 8, and say it's 'fun'. 2-9: Approachable. Drink 4, and say it's 'fun'. 10-19: You pour his '82 into a Solo cup. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You pour his '82 into a Solo cup. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Grape Elemental | `dungeon.grape-elemental` | challenge | Two tons of grapes, stomped into rage. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Stomped. Drink 8, then stomp your feet for 5 seconds. 2-9: Stomped. Drink 4, then stomp your feet for 5 seconds. 10-19: Raisin. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Raisin. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Cellar Master | `dungeon.cellar-master` | challenge | He knows every bottle, and you broke one. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He makes you sweep. Drink 8. 2-9: He makes you sweep. Drink 4. 10-19: You blame the cat. He believes you. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You blame the cat. He believes you. Give 8. |

| ☐ | Count Corkula | `dungeon.count-corkula` | challenge | He drinks pinot. Yours. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He drains your glass. Drink 8. 2-8: He drains your glass. Drink 4. 9-19: A stake through the cork. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! Sunlight. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Fangs out. Staring contest with your left neighbor, playing the Count. Blink first: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx The Count: The jinxed player counts their sips out loud, with a cackle. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Cask Queen | `dungeon.cask-queen` | challenge | She sits on a throne of barrels and judges your palate. Curtsy, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Off with your glass. Finish your drink. 2-8: Your palate is basic. Drink 4. 9-19: You knock out a barrel. She tumbles. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You knock out a barrel. She tumbles. Give 10. · Beat 2: She demands a toast. Give her one, ten seconds, no ums; the table judges. Fail: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Curtsy Court: The jinxed player curtsies before every drink. Forget: drink 1. |

Drink 8. 2-8: He sneers you to death. Drink 4. 9-19: You hand him a White Claw. He dissolves. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He dissolves into a nice rosé. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He sniffs your glass with contempt. Roll 2d6 to prove your taste. (2d6 — 2-6: Corked. Drink 4. 7-12: Notes of oak and victory. Give 4.) |

He spits your drink back in your glass. Drink 8. 2-8: He names your drink, your year and your ex. Drink 4. 9-19: He calls your boxed wine a Bordeaux. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He calls your boxed wine a Bordeaux. Give 10. · Beat 2: Blind tasting: eyes shut, sip a drink the table hands you. Name it wrong: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |

Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It rolls back together, bigger. Roll 2d6. (2d6 — 2-7: It absorbs your shoe. Drink 5. 8-12: You flush it. Give 4.) · Jinx Clogged: The jinxed player can't say 'I' or 'me'. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Alligator Al | `dungeon.alligator-al` | challenge | A twelve-foot gator in a bowling shirt. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Al doesn't like your face. Drink 10. 2-11: Al doesn't like your face. Drink 5. 12-19: You bowl him a strike. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Gutter ball, and he's in it. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Al wants your bowling stance. Hold it on one leg while the table counts to 15. Wobble down: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Plumber King | `dungeon.plumber-king` | challenge | Crowned in a toilet seat with a plunger for a scepter. He demands tribute. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He crowns you with the seat. Finish your drink. 2-11: Plunged. Drink 5. 12-19: You snap the plunger. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You snap the plunger. Give 8. · Beat 2: Kneel and swear fealty to the Plumber King, in one breath, in rhyme. Stumble: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Jinx Plunger Hands: Nobody points at anything. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Mama Gator | `dungeon.the-crocodile-mom` | challenge | Gator Junior's mother has heard what you did. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She adopts you. Finish your drink, then call her mom. 2-11: She sits on you, gently, for an hour. Drink 5. 12-19: You apologize to her face. She weeps. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You apologize to her face. She weeps. Give 8. · Beat 2: She wants an apology. Apologize to your left neighbor for 15 seconds, sincerely. Laugh: drink 4. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Pipe Worm | `dungeon.the-pipe-worm` | challenge | Eighty years in the plumbing, and now it's as long as the city. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It wraps around you twice. Drink 10. 2-11: It wraps around you twice. Drink 5. 12-19: You tie it in a knot. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You tie it in a bow. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: It comes up through the drain behind you. Roll d6. (1d6 — 1-1: It swallows your arm. Drink 5. 2-2: It swallows your drink. Drink 4. 3-3: It hisses. Drink 3. 4-4: You plug it. Give 3. 5-5: You snake it. Give 4. 6-6: You tie it to a pipe. Everyone else drinks 2.) · Jinx Pipe Down: Only the drawer may talk to the jinxed player. Anyone else does: they drink 1. |
| ☐ | Sommelier Kraken | `dungeon.sommelier-kraken` | challenge | The kraken sips from four glasses at once. Roll four dice. | 4d6 — Four glasses, four sips. You drink {first}; the next three clockwise drink {second}, {third} and {fourth}. |
| ☐ | Last Orders Siren | `dungeon.last-orders-siren` | challenge | A siren sings one more drink, and it's beautiful. Cover your ears, then roll d20 to resist. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You swim to her. Finish your drink. 2-10: You swim to her. Finish half your drink. 11-19: You stuff your ears with limes. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You stuff your ears with limes. Give 10. |
| ☐ | The Drowned Regular | `dungeon.drowned-regular` | challenge | Read alone his one story, start the clock, then tell it as him. The table guesses what it's about. | Secret: THE ONE THAT GOT AWAY (A FISH). · Clock 45 s: Time. Someone guessed it? Give 3. Nobody did? Drink 3. |
| ☐ | Foam Leviathan | `dungeon.foam-leviathan` | challenge | A head of foam the size of a city bus rises out of the taps. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It washes over you. Drink 10, and wear the mustache. 2-10: It washes over you. Drink 5, and wear the mustache. 11-19: You blow the head off. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You blow the head off. Give 10. |
| ☐ | Piranha Shots | `dungeon.piranha-shots` | challenge | Piranhas in the shots. On three, everyone points at who'd do one anyway. Most fingers: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Your Merfolk Ex | `dungeon.merfolk-ex` | challenge | Your ex is doing amazing. Out-brag your left neighbor for 15 seconds. Table's loser: drink 4. | Jinx Fish Out of Water: The jinxed player gasps like a fish after every sip. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Text-Them Eel | `dungeon.text-them-eel` | challenge | An eel whispers: text them. Hand your phone to the left, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You text them. Drink 8, and show the table your last text. 2-10: You text them. Drink 4, and show the table your last text. 11-19: You bite the eel first. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You text the eel instead. It leaves you on read. Give 10. · Jinx Typing...: The jinxed player says 'typing...' before they speak. Forgets: drink 1. |
| ☐ | Tap Mimic | `dungeon.tap-mimic` | challenge | Pour-off: you and your right neighbor mime a perfect pint. The table picks. Worse pour: drink 5. |  |

Drink 10. 2-10: He takes it in blood. Drink 5. 11-19: You hand him a rum and coke. He's confused, then grateful. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You hand him a rum and coke. He's confused, then grateful. Give 10. · Jinx Pirate Tongue: The jinxed player ends every sentence with 'arr'. Forget: drink 1. |

| ☐ | Hot Wort | `dungeon.hot-wort` | challenge | The floor is covered in hot, sticky wort. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Down you go. Drink 4. 2-8: Down you go. Drink 2. 9-19: You moonwalk across. Moonwalk to your seat, then give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You moonwalk across. Moonwalk to your seat, then give 4. |
| ☐ | The Brewery Tour | `dungeon.brewery-tour` | challenge | A tour group in hairnets wants a photo. Strike a pose, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You're in the brochure. Drink 4. 2-6: You're in the brochure. Drink 2. 7-19: You lead them into a vat. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You lead them into a vat. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Bottle Gnomes | `dungeon.bottle-gnomes` | challenge | Two gnomes stealing bottles saw you see them. Whistle innocently, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Hush money. Drink 4. 2-6: Hush money. Drink 2. 7-19: You take a cut. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You take a cut. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Spent Grain Golem | `dungeon.spent-grain-golem` | challenge | A grain golem wants a bakery. On three, point at who'd bake for it. Most fingers: give 2. |  |
| ☐ | Hazy Wraith | `dungeon.hazy-wraith` | challenge | A ghost so cloudy you can't see through it. It's 9 percent. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It haunts your palate. Drink 6. 2-6: It haunts your palate. Drink 3. 7-19: You hold it to the light. Give 3 to whoever's hazy right now. 20-20: Natural 20! You hold it to the light. Give 6 to whoever's hazy right now. |
| ☐ | Forklift Goblin | `dungeon.forklift-goblin` | challenge | A goblin learned to drive a forklift. He has not learned to stop. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: A pallet to the shins. Drink 3. 2-2: He reverses over your foot. Drink 3. 3-3: You dodge. Give 2. 4-4: You take the keys. Give 3. 5-5: You wave him into a vat. Give 3. 6-6: You ride the forks. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Quality Control | `dungeon.quality-control` | challenge | Say 'the brewer brewed better beer' five times fast. Trip up: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Loose Hose | `dungeon.loose-hose` | challenge | A wash-down hose is loose and whipping across the floor. Roll d20 to jump it. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It wraps your ankles. Drink 4. 2-8: It wraps your ankles. Drink 2. 9-19: You stamp it flat. Give 2 to whoever jumped. 20-20: Natural 20! You stamp it flat. Give 4 to whoever jumped. |
| ☐ | Homebrew Club | `dungeon.homebrew-club` | challenge | Four beards with growlers want your opinion. All of it. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You give it. Drink 4. 2-6: You give it. Drink 2. 7-19: You say 'it's very you' and they leave. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You say 'it's very you' and they leave. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Brewmaster | `dungeon.the-brewmaster` | challenge | Forty years at the kettle, and you just called his stout 'fine'. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He makes you drink the whole flight. Drink 8. 2-9: He makes you drink the whole flight. Drink 4. 10-19: You call it his best. He weeps. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You call it his best. He weeps. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Barrel-Aged Horror | `dungeon.barrel-aged-horror` | challenge | Something has been aging in barrel 9 for twelve years. It's out. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's very strong. Drink 8. 2-9: It's very strong. Drink 4. 10-19: You cork it back in. Give 4 to the oldest player here. 20-20: Natural 20! You cork it back in. Give 8 to the oldest player here. |
| ☐ | The Hostile Takeover | `dungeon.hostile-brewer` | challenge | A rival brewery is taking over the taps, one tap handle at a time. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They take your tap. Drink 8. 2-9: They take your tap. Drink 4. 10-19: You take their tap. Take someone's drink for a sip; they drink 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take their tap. Take someone's drink for a sip; they drink 8. |
| ☐ | The Head Brewer | `dungeon.head-brewer` | challenge | She's tasted every batch since 1998, and she hates yours. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She pours it out in front of you. Drink 8. 2-9: She pours it out in front of you. Drink 4. 10-19: She asks for the recipe. Make one up out loud, then give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! She asks for the recipe. Make one up out loud, then give 8. |
| ☐ | The Fermenter | `dungeon.the-fermenter` | challenge | A forty-foot fermentation tank, under pressure, and it's angry. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It blows its lid on you. Drink 8. 2-8: It blows its lid on you. Drink 4. 9-19: You open the valve. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You drink it down to the yeast. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: The pressure's building. Hold one note while the table counts to ten. Run out of air: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | King Hop | `dungeon.king-hop` | challenge | A hop cone the size of a car. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You call it malty. Finish your drink. 2-8: Too bitter. Drink 4. 9-19: You dry-hop the king. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You dry-hop the king. Give 10. · Beat 2: Describe your drink like a beer snob for 15 seconds. Laugh or stall: drink 3. (Won/Lost) |
| ☐ | The Beer Snob Lich | `dungeon.beer-snob-lich` | challenge | Undead, and still telling everyone about Belgian monks. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He lectures you to death. Drink 8. 2-8: He lectures you to death. Drink 4. 9-19: You fall asleep, and it kills him. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You out-snob him. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: Name beers back and forth with your left neighbor, playing the Lich. Stall or repeat first: drink 3. (Won/Lost) · Jinx A Belgian: Nobody says 'beer'; it's 'a Belgian'. Slip: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The Keg Kraken | `dungeon.keg-kraken` | challenge | Eight arms, each one a tap. Side bets: thumbs up or down; wrong bets drink 1. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It pours down your throat. Finish your drink. 2-8: Eight pours at once. Drink 4. 9-19: You tie its taps in a knot. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You tie its taps in a knot. Give 10. · Beat 2: All eight taps open at once. Roll 2d6 to shut them. (2d6 — 2-6: Soaked. Drink 4. 7-12: You close the main valve. Give 4.) |
| ☐ | The Brewery Owner | `dungeon.brewery-owner` | challenge | He just sold to a conglomerate and he's celebrating on you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He sprays you with champagne. Drink 8. 2-8: He sprays you with champagne. Drink 4. 9-19: You read the contract. He owes you. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You buy it back. Everyone else drinks 3. · Beat 2: He offers you a job at the brewery. Roll d6 to negotiate. (1d6 — 1-1: Unpaid internship. Drink 4. 2-2: Paid in exposure. Drink 3. 3-3: Free beer for life. Give 3. 4-4: Equity. Give 3. 5-5: You buy the brewery. Give 4. 6-6: You fire him. Everyone else drinks 2.) · Jinx Artisanal: The jinxed player calls everything 'artisanal'. Misses a chance the table spots: drink 1. |

You're cut off. Drink 8, then water. 2-8: He pours you a glacier. Drink 4. 9-19: You tip him. He warms right up. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You tip him. He warms right up. Give 8. |

| ☐ | The Pigeon Squadron | `dungeon.pigeon-squadron` | challenge | Twelve pigeons in a V, on a strafing run. Duck, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Direct hit. Drink 8. 2-10: Direct hit. Drink 4. 11-19: You duck behind the bar. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You duck behind the bar. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Rogue Drone | `dungeon.rogue-drone` | challenge | A rogue drone buzzes the table, filming. Roll four dice. | 4d6 — It films four bad sides. You drink {first}; the next three clockwise drink {second}, {third} and {fourth}. |
| ☐ | The Hot Tub Wizard | `dungeon.hot-tub-wizard` | challenge | A hot tub 'wizard' wants your drink. Hold it close, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He's a wizard. Drink 10. 2-10: He's a wizard. Drink 5. 11-19: He's just an old man. Give 5 to whoever believed him. 20-20: Natural 20! He is a wizard, and he likes you. Give 10, and he makes it rain. |

Drink 4. 13-19: You poke it from a safe distance. Point at someone; they drink 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You poke it from a safe distance. Point at someone; they drink 6. |

Drink 8. 2-10: You're in the album forever. Drink 4. 11-19: You show them a better spot. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You show them a better spot. Give 8. |

Drink 12. 2-13: He has you thrown off the guest list. Drink 6. 14-19: You buy the floor below his. Give 6 to the richest-looking player here. 20-20: Natural 20! You buy the floor below his. Give 12 to the richest-looking player here. |

Drink 12. 2-13: Stone fist. Drink 6. 14-19: You crown yourself. Give 6, split among your subjects. 20-20: Natural 20! You crown yourself. Give 12, split among your subjects. |

Drink 6. 14-19: You ground him. Touch someone's shoulder, zap; they drink 6. 20-20: Natural 20! You ground him. Touch someone's shoulder, zap; they drink 12. |
| ☐ | The Helicopter Pilot | `dungeon.helicopter-pilot` | challenge | He landed on the roof and won't leave until somebody pays. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You're paying. Drink 12. 2-13: You're paying. Drink 6. 14-19: Free ride. Pick a co-pilot; you both give 6. 20-20: Natural 20! Free ride. Pick a co-pilot; you both give 12. |
| ☐ | The Last Call Lich | `dungeon.last-call-lich` | challenge | A bony hand rings the bell. It's last call forever unless you kill it. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Closing time. Everyone drinks 8. 2-12: Closing time. Everyone drinks 4. 13-19: It flickers. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! The music comes back on. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: It rings the bell. Out-shout your left neighbor: 'One more!' The table judges. Lose: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: One bony hand left on the bell. Everyone raises a glass. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The bell rings. Everyone drinks 8. 2-12: The bell rings. Everyone drinks 4. 13-19: You take the bell. Everyone else drinks 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You take the bell. Everyone else drinks 8.) |
| ☐ | The Bartender Below | `dungeon.bartender-below` | challenge | It knows your usual. It knows what you did. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It cuts you off. Drink 12. 2-12: It cuts you off. Drink 6. 13-19: You tip it. It staggers. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You rob the tip jar. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: It wants your order in three seconds, with a straight face. The table judges. Fail: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: It reaches for the cut-off bell. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Cut off. Finish your drink, then a fresh one. 2-12: Cut off. Finish your drink. 13-19: You tip big. It weeps. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You tip big. It weeps. Give 10.) |
| ☐ | The Final Tab | `dungeon.final-tab` | challenge | Every drink you've ever ordered, itemized, four feet long, and it has teeth. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Itemized. Drink 10. 2-12: Itemized. Drink 5. 13-19: You dispute a charge. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! Comped. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: Split the bill: say what everyone at the table drank tonight, in ten seconds. Miss one: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: The total comes in. It's a lot. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You pay. Everyone drinks 8. 2-12: You pay. Everyone drinks 4. 13-19: You dine and dash. Give 6. 20-20: Natural 20! You dine and dash. Give 12.) |
| ☐ | The House Lights | `dungeon.house-lights` | challenge | The lights come up, and everyone looks like that. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You saw yourself. Drink 12. 2-12: You saw yourself. Drink 6. 13-19: You smash a bulb. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! Darkness again. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: Everyone looks at you. Strike a pose and hold it while the table counts to ten. Break: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: The last bulb flickers. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Full brightness. Everyone drinks 8. 2-12: Full brightness. Everyone drinks 4. 13-19: Darkness. Everyone else drinks 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Darkness. Everyone else drinks 8.) · Jinx Lit Up: The jinxed player squints at everyone. Caught with their eyes wide open: drink 1. |
| ☐ | The 2 a.m. Kebab | `dungeon.two-am-kebab` | challenge | It's been turning since 8 p.m. It knew you'd come. It's the size of a door. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You drop it on the floor and eat it anyway. Drink 10. 2-12: Garlic sauce, everywhere. Drink 5. 13-19: You carve it down to the spit. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the whole thing, standing. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: Order for the table in one breath: what everyone wants on theirs. Stumble: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: It's down to the spit, and it's still turning. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Garlic sauce in your eye. Drink 10. 2-12: Garlic sauce in your eye. Drink 5. 13-19: You eat the last bite. Give 6. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the last bite. Give 12.) |

Drink 10. 2-12: Surge goes up again. Drink 5. 13-19: You find a driver who's going your way. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! Free ride. Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: Haggle a fare with your left neighbor, playing the driver, for ten seconds. They refuse: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: It's three minutes away, forever. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Ride cancelled. Everyone drinks 8. 2-12: Ride cancelled. Everyone drinks 4. 13-19: Five stars. Give 6. 20-20: Natural 20! Five stars. Give 12.) · Jinx Surge: The jinxed player adds 'plus tax' to every drink they're given. Forgets: drink 1. |

Everyone else drinks 4. · Beat 2: Read your last sent text out loud to the table. Refuse: drink 4. (Won/Lost) · Beat 3: Someone's typing. Roll d20. (1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's the video. | ☐ | Flooded Stairs | `dungeon.omen-flooded-stairs` | rule | Last to say 'glub' when a floor opens drinks 1. Traps cost a heart more. Take a smoke bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Happy Hour | `dungeon.omen-happy-hour` | rule | Two for one: whoever drinks picks someone to drink with them. Every heal heals a heart more. |  |
| ☐ | Running a Tab | `dungeon.omen-running-a-tab` | rule | Say 'on my tab' when handed a drink, or drink 1. Two hearts short, with a shield and a draught. |  |
| ☐ | The Lock-In | `dungeon.omen-lock-in` | rule | Ask before leaving your seat, or drink 2. Bosses take a hit more. Take a die and a shield. |  |
| ☐ | Cargo Shorts | `dungeon.omen-cargo-shorts` | rule | You wore cargo shorts: drink 3. One more satchel slot, and a draught in a pocket. |  |
| ☐ | Butterfingers | `dungeon.omen-butterfingers` | rule | Anyone who drops anything drinks 2. One satchel slot fewer, but two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Blood Moon | `dungeon.omen-blood-moon` | rule | Howl at every monster; last to howl drinks 1. Fights cost a heart more, but two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Lefty Night | `dungeon.omen-lefty-night` | rule | Everyone drinks with their off hand all crawl. Caught using the other: drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Karaoke Night | `dungeon.omen-karaoke-night` | rule | Before every boss swing, the drawer sings one line of anything. Refuse: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Regulars | `dungeon.omen-the-regulars` | rule | Everyone shouts the drawer's name when they draw, like a regular. Forget: drink 1. Two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Thin Walls | `dungeon.omen-thin-walls` | rule | Whisper the whole crawl; too loud, drink 1. Trials cost a heart more, but heals heal one more. |  |
| ☐ | The Pregame | `dungeon.omen-cold-open` | rule | Everyone drinks 1 at the door. Start a heart short, with a loaded die and a shield. |  |
| ☐ | Payday | `dungeon.omen-payday` | rule | Everyone got paid today. Gambles and relics roll 2 higher. Start a heart short. |  |
| ☐ | Designated Driver | `dungeon.omen-designated-driver` | rule | The DD packed a draught. One more satchel slot, but bosses cost a heart more. |  |
| ☐ | Friday the 13th | `dungeon.omen-friday-13th` | rule | Bad luck and good luck: natural 1s land on a 2 too, natural 20s on a 19. |  |
| ☐ | Trivia Night | `dungeon.omen-trivia-night` | rule | Before each boss the drawer answers a trivia question, or drinks 2. Trials cost less, traps more. |  |
| ☐ | Brad's Birthday | `dungeon.omen-brads-birthday` | rule | Brad's birthday: the Quarter-Zips are feral. Ambushes cost a heart more. Take a shield. |  |
| ☐ | Open Bar | `dungeon.omen-open-bar` | rule | A wedding upstairs has an open bar. Take two draughts, but one satchel slot fewer. |  |
| ☐ | Name Tags | `dungeon.omen-name-tags` | rule | Everyone picks a fantasy name at the door and answers only to it. Wrong name: drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Lucky Penny | `dungeon.omen-lucky-penny` | rule | A lucky penny on the stairs. Every d20 fight rolls 1 higher, but start two hearts short. |  |
| ☐ | Barbarian | `dungeon.class-barbarian` | rule | Rage first. Questions never. +2 to boss d20s. Traps hurt 1 more. Wears Rusty Greataxe. |  |
| ☐ | Rogue | `dungeon.class-rogue` | rule | Has your wallet. +2 to trap and relic d20s. +1 satchel slot. Starts with a Smoke Bomb. Wears Shiv. |  |
| ☐ | Cleric | `dungeon.class-cleric` | rule | Blesses every drink, loudly. Every heal heals 1 more. Wears Holy Vestments. |  |
| ☐ | Wizard | `dungeon.class-wizard` | rule | Reads the menu like a spellbook. −2 max hearts. Starts with 2 Loaded Dice. Wears Pointy Hat. |  |
| ☐ | Bard | `dungeon.class-bard` | rule | Brought a guitar. Nobody asked. +1 max heart. Natural 20 on a 19 or 20. Wears Battered Lute. |  |
| ☐ | Paladin | `dungeon.class-paladin` | rule | Sworn to guard the table's drinks. +1 max heart. Starts with a Coaster Shield. Wears Holy Mace. |  |
| ☐ | Ranger | `dungeon.class-ranger` | rule | Tracks the bar by smell. +2 to monster d20s. Bosses hurt 1 more. Wears Bar Darts. |  |
| ☐ | Drunken Monk | `dungeon.class-monk` | rule | Starts drunk. Gets better. −1 max heart. Natural 1 on a 1 or 2. Wears Hip Flask. |  |
| ☐ | Necromancer | `dungeon.class-necromancer` | rule | Has died twice tonight already. −3 max hearts. Starts with a Second Wind. Wears Bone Wand. |  |
| ☐ | Alchemist | `dungeon.class-alchemist` | rule | Has a flask for every flask. +1 max heart. +1 satchel slot. Starts with 2 Healing Draughts. |  |
| ☐ | Warlock | `dungeon.class-warlock` | rule | Sold their soul for a better seat. −3 max hearts. +2 to every battle d20. Wears Hellfire Hoodie. |  |
| ☐ | Bouncer | `dungeon.class-bouncer` | rule | Checked your ID twice. +2 max hearts. Ambushes hurt 1 less. Wears Heavy Flashlight. |  |
| ☐ | Bartender | `dungeon.class-bartender` | rule | Knows everyone's order. +1 max heart. +1 satchel slot. Starts with a Bar Tab and a Healing Draught. |  |
| ☐ | Mercenary | `dungeon.class-mercenary` | rule | Will fight anything for a drink. +1 to monster d20s. +3 to elite d20s. Wears Kevlar Vest. |  |
| ☐ | Druid | `dungeon.class-druid` | rule | Smells like moss. Talks to the plants. +1 max heart. +2 to trap d20s. Wears Pet Ferret. |  |
| ☐ | The Plus-One | `dungeon.class-plus-one` | rule | Nobody invited them. −3 max hearts. −1 to boss d20s. Every 20 is rerolled. Wears Borrowed Blazer. |  |
| ☐ | The Intern | `dungeon.class-intern` | rule | Unpaid, sadly. −2 max hearts. Natural 1 on a 1 or 2. −2 satchel slots. Wears Visitor Lanyard. |  |
| ☐ | The Mimic | `dungeon.class-mimic` | rule | A very confused chest. −5 max hearts. −1 to every battle d20. +2 satchel slots. Wears Its Own Lid. |  |

## Notes for review

- Dice cards are ordinary cards with a `dice` definition (which dice plus how
  to read the total); the engine pauses the deck until the roll is resolved.
- Secret cards show the secret only while a player holds the card's plaque;
  a fuse burns for a hidden time in its range, a clock counts down in view,
  and the card can't be put aside until time is up.
- Rules target 90 characters / 18 words; the build warns above that and
  rejects over 100 characters / 20 words.
- Rank cards for 9 / 10 / Jack / Queen are intentionally covered by the
  existing Rhyme Time, Categories, Rulemaster and Questions Only cards.
